Joe believes his hospital's babies weigh more on average than those from Green Veggies.</p><p>Use a one tailed two sample t test for H1: &mu;<sub>Joe</sub> &gt; &mu;<sub>Veggies</sub>.</p><p>Assume unequal variances (Welch's t test).</p><table style='border-collapse:collapse;'><tr><td style='vertical-align:top; padding-right:20px;'><b>Joe's Hospital (lbs):</b><br/><span style='font-family:monospace; background-color:#eee; white-space:pre;'>7.5<br/>7.0<br/>9.3<br/>6.4<br/>8.1<br/>8.0<br/>5.3<br/>8.7<br/>8.5<br/>6.6<br/>8.0<br/>5.7<br/>7.7<br/>6.0<br/>9.3<br/>6.6<br/>7.5<br/>8.7<br/>6.5<br/>9.1<br/>7.3<br/>9.0<br/>10.3<br/>5.9<br/>6.1<br/>8.9<br/>7.6<br/>7.6<br/>9.2<br/>9.8<br/>6.1<br/>8.2<br/>8.0<br/>8.9<br/>5.6</span></td><td style='vertical-align:top;'><b>Green Veggies (lbs):</b><br/><span style='font-family:monospace; background-color:#eee; white-space:pre;'>7.9<br/>9.8<br/>6.5<br/>7.5<br/>10.2<br/>4.7<br/>7.9<br/>4.7<br/>8.1<br/>8.3<br/>6.6<br/>6.1<br/>9.2<br/>8.3<br/>7.0<br/>11.2<br/>7.8<br/>7.6<br/>9.0<br/>7.3<br/>8.7<br/>5.4<br/>9.3<br/>6.0<br/>5.5<br/>5.9<br/>8.0<br/>8.4<br/>8.5<br/>7.8<br/>7.9<br/>7.9</span></td></tr></table><p>Compute the p value in Google Sheets using the tutorial: <a href='https://docs.google.com/document/d/1k5UzUlpu-5wCMyWpop0bFlXBgAWivwTwd020Jgo1EIk/edit' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>link here</a>.</p><p>Enter your result as a decimal between 0 and 1 (for example, 0.084, not 8.4%).</p>	0.46677929	0.00470000
